```
def soft_obj_z(z, alpha):
  h = sym_full(z)
  c = np.correlate(h, 1-h,'full')
  m = c.max()
  shifted = alpha*(c-m)
  smax = m + np.logsumexp(shifted)
  return (c.max() + logsumexp(alpha*(c-c.max()))/alpha)*dx
```

Pseudo:
```
def solve_coord(c, h, p, n, tol=1e-12, max_bisect=40):
    h0 = h[p]
    lag_lo = min(-p, p - (n - 1))
    lag_hi = max(n - 1 - p, p)
    ms = np.arange(n - 1 + lag_lo, n - 1 + lag_hi + 1)  # length L
    lag = ms - (n - 1)
    # first valid: second = p + lag
    jf = p + lag
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    # second valid: first = p - lag
    jb = p - lag
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    a = np.zeros(len(ms))
    if any vf: a += 1 - h[jf[vf]]
    if any vb: a -= h[jb[vb]]
    diag_pos = lag == 0  # only one bool array; index maybe
    has_diag = any diag_pos
    if has_diag:
        d_idx = np.nonzero(diag_pos)[0][0]
        c_diag = c[ms[d_idx]]
    c_aff = c[ms]
    # unaffected max. Since ms may cover large but not all if p middle; need max of c outside ms.
    # To avoid boolean full length each coordinate? full length 1399; okay maybe but per coordinate cost. We can compute unaff efficiently:
    # unaff = max(c[:ms[0]], c[ms[-1]+1:]) if ms contiguous.
    unaff = 0.0? If vector all ones? c values nonnegative? csum = sum h(1-h) >=0, so min 0. Use 0 if no outside.
    if ms[0] > 0: unaff = max(unaff, c[:ms[0]].max())
    if ms[-1] < 2*n-2: unaff = max(unaff, c[ms[-1]+1:].max())
    cur_max = np.max(c)
    Llo = unaff
    Lhi = cur_max
    # If h0 at 0 or1 maybe still can move? Yes solve can move from boundary. If bounds degenerate? If h0=0, x∈[0,1]. Fine.
```

```

```
lo=0; hi=1
if any zero_c > L+tol: infeasible

if any neg: lower = h0 + (L - c_aff[neg]) / a[neg]; lo = max(lo, lower.min())
```

def coordinate_descent(h, c, n, max_passes, deadline, rng, damp=0.5, random_order=False, accept_tol=1e-12, max_damp_dec=...):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    constant=n/2.0
    for it in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       old_h=h.copy(); old_c=c.copy()
       order = rng.permutation(n) if random_order else np.arange(n)
       changed=False
       for p in order:
           if time.time()>deadline+? break? Need stop pass if deadline; but if incomplete projection maybe accept partial? Better if near deadline, break and recompute.
           xfull = solve_coord(c, h, p, n)
           delta = damp*(xfull - h[p])
           if abs(delta) > 1e-12:
              apply_update ... changed=True
       if time left insufficient to project/recompute: break? We can project and evaluate.
       h = project_sum_box_old? Actually project on h after pass.
       c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       cur = max(c)*dx
       if cur < best_c - 1e-14:
           best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
       else:
           # revert to old if worse than current? Maybe keep current h? We want not deteriorate.
           h=old_h; c=old_c
           damp *= 0.5
           if damp < 0.1: damp=0.1
       # if no changed break
       if not changed: break
    return best_h,best_c
```

Potential issue: project_sum_box via water-filling changes values by small τ; if old h sum =target, after damped coordinate updates sum drift maybe moderate. Bisection tau [-1,1] ok. But if h has values near 0/1 and τ push, okay. Need adjust exact sum after bisection. Implement:
```
def project_sum_box(h, target):
   x = np.clip(h,0,1)
   lo,hi=-1.0,1.0
   for _ in range(80):
      mid=(lo+hi)/2
      if np.clip(x-mid,0,1).sum() > target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   y=np.clip(x-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
   # fine adjust
   for _ in range(50):
      diff=target-y.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
      if diff>0: mask=y<1-1e-15; if not: break; y[mask]+=diff/mask.sum(); y=np.minimum(y,1)
      else: mask=y>1e-15; if not: break; y[mask]+=diff/mask.sum(); y=np.maximum(y,0)
   return y
```
But the fine adjustment additive may overshoot; use small. Since bisection high precision, residual tiny.

Actually bisection f(tau)=sum clip(x-tau) decreasing. If val > target, need increase tau -> lo=mid. If val < target, need decrease tau -> hi=mid. After 80, tau within 2^-80 ~1e-24. Good. Residual maybe rounding. Fine adjust.

def update_coord(c,h,p,n,damp, max_bisect=32):
    h0 = h[p]
    lag_lo = min(p - (n-1), -p)
    lag_hi = max(p, n-1-p)
    ms = np.arange(n-1+lag_lo, n-1+lag_hi+1)
    lag = ms - (n-1)
    a = np.zeros(len(ms))
    jf = p - lag
    vf = (jf>=0)&(jf<n)
    if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    jb = p + lag
    vb = (jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    diag_mask = (lag == 0)
    if any: d_idx = int(np.argmax(diag_mask)) (only one)
                                c_diag = c[ms[d_idx]]
    c_aff = c[ms].copy()? Need c_aff read; we can use c[ms] view? It's a copy? c[ms] with fancy index returns copy. We'll need for bisection.
    # precompute masks
    pos = a > 1e-15; neg = a < -1e-15; zero = ~(pos|neg)
    zero_c = c_aff[zero]
    # unaff
    unaff=0.0
    if ms[0] > 0: unaff = float(c[:ms[0]].max())
    last=ms[-1]
    if last < 2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff, float(c[last+1:].max()))
    Llo = unaff; Lhi = float(np.max(c)) # Could use max(unaff, max(c_aff)) but c max outside.
    # If Llo > Lhi? no.
    for _ in range(max_bisect):
       L = 0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       feasible = True
       if zero_c.size and zero_c.max() > L + 1e-14: feasible=False
       if feasible and pos.any():
          # upper = h0 + (L - c_aff[pos]) / a[pos]
          up = h0 + (L - c_aff[pos]) / a[pos]
          hi = float(np.min(up))
          if hi < 0: hi=0? Actually if min upper <0, hi<0 -> infeasible since x<0. But we will clip; set feasible if hi < -tol.
       ...
```

       hi = 1.0
       if pos.any():
          hi = min(hi, float(np.min(h0 + (L - c_aff[pos])/a[pos])))
       lo = 0.0
       if neg.any():
          lo = max(lo, float(np.max(h0 + (L - c_aff[neg])/a[neg])))
       if lo > hi + 1e-14: feasible=False
       # diag:
       if feasible and has_diag:
          Cq = L - c_diag + h0*(1-h0)
          if Cq < -1e-14: feasible=False
          elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-14:
             r1 = (1 - np.sqrt(max(0,1-4*Cq)))*0.5
             r2 = 1 - r1
             # interval [lo,hi] must intersect [0,r1] or [r2,1]
             if not ((hi >= 0 and lo <= r1 + 1e-14) or (hi >= r2 - 1e-14 and lo <= 1)):

       else: Llo=L
```

Choose_x:
```
def choose_x(lo, hi, h0, diag_info):
   lo=max(0,lo); hi=min(1,hi)
   if lo > hi: return h0
   if diag_info:
      Cq, r1, r2
      if Cq <= 1e-14:
          # endpoints feasible
          cand=[]
          if lo <= 1e-12 <= hi: cand.append(0)
          if lo <= 1-1e-12 <= hi: cand.append(1)
          if cand: return min(cand, key=lambda v: abs(v-h0)) # no lambda! use if
          return h0 if lo<=h0<=hi else (lo if h0<lo else hi)
      elif Cq >=0.25-1e-12:
          return min(max(h0,lo),hi)
      else:
          if hi <= r1 + 1e-12:
             return min(max(h0,lo),hi)
          if lo >= r2 - 1e-12:
             return min(max(h0,lo),hi)
          # spans gap
          hi1 = min(hi, r1)
          lo2 = max(lo, r2)
          best = None
          if hi1 >= lo - 1e-12:
             x1 = min(max(h0, lo), max(lo, hi1)) # clip h0 to [lo,hi1]
             # but if h0 < lo, clip to lo, which <=hi1 if hi1>=lo
          if lo2 <= hi + 1e-12:
             x2 = min(max(h0, lo2), hi)
          choose closer to h0
```

import numpy as np, time

def compute_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h, mode='full'))*dx)

def project_sum_box(x, target):
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=float),0,1)
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.clip(x-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(x-(lo+hi)*0.5,0,1)
    for _ in range(50):
       diff=target-y.sum()
       if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
       if diff > 0:
          mask=y<1.0
          if not np.any(mask): break
          y[mask] += diff / np.count_nonzero(mask)
          y[np.where(y>1.0)[0]] = 1.0? Use np.minimum
       else:
          mask=y>0.0
          if not np.any(mask): break
          y[mask] += diff / np.count_nonzero(mask)
          y=np.maximum(y,0)
    return y
```

def score_candidate(x, c_aff, a, d_idx, c_diag, h0, floor, has_diag):
    delta=x-h0
    cnew = c_aff + a*delta
    if has_diag:
       cnew[d_idx] = c_diag + a[d_idx]*delta - delta*delta
    # use active mask maybe precomputed? We can just sum positive excess over floor for all cnew
    excess = cnew - floor
    return float(np.sum(np.maximum(excess,0.0)))
```

Detailed `update_coord` implementation without secondary:
```
def update_coord(c, h, p, n, damp, bisect=32):
    h0 = h[p]
    lag_lo = min(p - (n-1), -p)
    lag_hi = max(p, n-1-p)
    ms = np.arange(n-1+lag_lo, n-1+lag_hi+1)
    lag = ms - (n-1)
    a = np.zeros(ms.size)
    jf = p - lag
    vf = (jf >=0) & (jf < n)
    if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    jb = p + lag
    vb = (jb >=0) & (jb < n)
    if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    d_idx = -1
    c_diag = 0.0
    zero_diag = (lag == 0)
    if np.any(zero_diag):
        d_idx = int(np.nonzero(zero_diag)[0][0])
        c_diag = c[ms[d_idx]]
    c_aff = c[ms]  # copy
    unaff = 0.0
    if ms[0] > 0: unaff = float(np.max(c[:ms[0]]))
    if ms[-1] < 2*n-2: unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[ms[-1]+1:])))
    cur_max = float(np.max(c))
    Llo = unaff
    Lhi = cur_max
    pos_mask = a > 1e-16
    neg_mask = a < -1e-16
    zero_mask = ~(pos_mask | neg_mask)
    zero_max = float(np.max(c_aff[zero_mask])) if np.any(zero_mask) else -np.inf
    pos_c = c_aff[pos_mask]; pos_a = a[pos_mask]
    neg_c = c_aff[neg_mask]; neg_a = a[neg_mask]
    for _ in range(bisect):
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       feasible=True
       if zero_mask.any() and zero_max > L + 1e-13:
          feasible=False
       if feasible:
          hi=1.0; lo=0.0
          if pos_mask.any():
             hi = float(np.min(h0 + (L - pos_c)/pos_a))
             if hi < 0: hi = -1e9
          if neg_mask.any():
             lo = float(np.max(h0 + (L - neg_c)/neg_a))
             if lo > 1: lo = 1e9
          lo = max(0.0, lo); hi = min(1.0, hi)
          if lo > hi + 1e-13: feasible=False
       if feasible and d_idx >=0:
          Cq = L - c_diag + h0*(1-h0)
          if Cq < -1e-13: feasible=False
          elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-13:
             disc = 1.0 - 4.0*Cq
             sqrt_disc = np.sqrt(disc) if disc > 0 else 0.0
             r1 = 0.5*(1.0 - sqrt_disc); r2 = 1.0 - r1
             if not ((hi >= 0.0 and lo <= r1 + 1e-13) or (hi >= r2 - 1e-13 and lo <= 1.0)):
                 feasible = False
       if feasible: Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    # compute interval final
    feasible=True
    hi=1.0; lo=0.0
    if zero_max > L + 1e-12: feasible=False
    if feasible and pos_mask.any():
       hi = float(np.min(h0 + (L - pos_c)/pos_a))
    if feasible and neg_mask.any():
       lo = float(np.max(h0 + (L - neg_c)/neg_a))
    lo=max(0,lo); hi=min(1,hi)
    if feasible and lo>hi+1e-10: feasible=False
    Cq = None; r1=r2=None
    if feasible and d_idx >=0:
       Cq = L - c_diag + h0*(1-h0)
       if Cq < -1e-10: feasible=False
       elif Cq < 0.25-1e-12:
          disc=1-4*Cq; sd=np.sqrt(disc) if disc>0 else 0; r1=0.5*(1-sd); r2=1-r1
          if not ((hi >= 0 and lo <= r1+1e-10) or (hi >= r2-1e-10 and lo <= 1)): feasible=False
    if not feasible:
       return 0.0
    x=clip_val(h0, lo, hi)
    if feasible and d_idx >=0 and Cq is not None and Cq < 0.25-1e-12:
       # if x in gap, choose nearest root
       if x > r1 + 1e-10 and x < r2 - 1e-10:
          if abs(r1 - x) <= abs(r2 - x): x = clip_val(r1, lo, hi)
          else: x = clip_val(r2, lo, hi)
       # Also if interval spans gap and h0 in one side but clip h0 could be in gap? handle above.
    delta = damp * (x - h0)
    if abs(delta) < 1e-14: return 0.0
    c[ms] += a * delta
    if d_idx >=0:
       c[ms[d_idx]] -= delta*delta
    h[p] = h0 + delta
    return delta
```
Issue: If h0 not in [lo,hi], clip picks nearest endpoint. If Cq gap and x after clip is endpoint but endpoint may be in gap? Clipping to [lo,hi] doesn't guarantee diag feasible if interval spans gap and h0 in gap? We handle if x in gap. If lo=0,hi=1, h0=0.5, r1=0.1,r2=0.9: x=0.5 in gap, choose closer root 0.1 or0.9. Good. If lo=0.4,hi=0.6 entirely inside gap, feasibility would have been false because interval intersects neither segment? Condition (hi>=0 and lo<=r1) false (lo=0.4>0.1); (hi>=r2) false (hi=0.6<0.9). So no update. If interval spans gap, lo< r1< r2 <hi, h0 gap -> choose root. Good.

But for Cq small <=1e-12, feasible points {0,1}. Our diag gap code with r1~0,r2~1; if hi whole interval, x maybe h0 in (0,1), gap (0,1) choose closer endpoint. If interval [0.2,0.3] not feasible? Now, `coordinate_descent` with random order:
```
def coordinate_descent(h_initial, c_initial, n, end_deadline, rng, random_order=False, damp_start=1.0, max_passes=10000, accept_tol=1e-14):
    h=np.array(h_initial,copy=True); c=np.array(c_initial,copy=True)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=max(c)* (2.0/n)
    target=n/2.0
    damp=damp_start
    min_damp=0.05
    no_improve=0
    while time.time() < end_deadline - 2:
        if np.max(c)*dx >= best_c - 1e-14: # current maybe best
        old_h=h.copy(); old_c=c.copy(); old_best=best_c
        start_c = float(np.max(c))*dx
        order = rng.permutation(n) if random_order else np.arange(n)
        changed=False
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > end_deadline - 2: break
            delta = update_coord(c,h,p,n,damp,32)
            if abs(delta)>1e-14: changed=True
        if time.time() > end_deadline - 6:
            break  # discard pass, keep best
        h = project_sum_box(h, target)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full')
        new_c = float(np.max(c))*dx
        if new_c < start_c - 1e-14:
            # accepted
            damp = min(1.0, damp*1.0)
            no_improve=0
            if new_c < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = new_c; best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            h=old_h; c=old_c
            damp = max(min_damp, damp*0.5)
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 3 and damp <= min_damp+1e-12:
                break
        if not changed:
            break
    return best_h,best_c
```
If a pass has no coordinate changed but due to min_damp? break.

If current path worsens relative global best but improves locally, we might continue from it. That's okay for restarts. `project_sum_box` fine adjustment: Need ensure if diff positive and mask all y<1 but adding diff/mask.sum() may exceed 1 for some? Since diff/cnt = (target - sum)/cnt. Use 100 bisections over [-1,1] gives tau precision 1e-30; floating sum error maybe ~1e-13. Fine adjust with proportional to room good.
```
def project_sum_box(x, target):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = -1.0; hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(100):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target: lo = mid
        else: hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(30):
        d = target - y.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-14: break
        if d > 0:
            mask = y < 1.0
            cnt = np.count_nonzero(mask)
            if cnt == 0: break
            y[mask] += d / cnt
            y = np.minimum(y, 1.0)
        else:
            mask = y > 0.0
            cnt = np.count_nonzero(mask)
            if cnt == 0: break
            y[mask] += d / cnt
            y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    return y
```
If `x` integer and target, bisection may find tau; fine.

`choose_diag_x`:
```
def choose_diag_x(lo, hi, h0, Cq):
    if lo is None:
        return clip_val(h0, 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = max(0.0, min(hi, lo)); hi = max(lo, hi)
    if lo > hi: return h0
    if Cq is None or Cq >= 0.25 - 1e-13:
        return clip_val(h0, lo, hi)
    tol=1e-12
    if Cq <= tol:
        best = None; bestd = 1e100
        for pt in (0.0,1.0):
            if lo - tol <= pt <= hi + tol:
                d=abs(pt - h0)
                if d < bestd: bestd=d; best=pt
        if best is not None: return best
        return clip_val(h0, lo, hi)
    disc = 1.0 - 4.0*Cq
    if disc < 0: disc=0.0
    sqrt_disc = np.sqrt(disc)
    r1 = 0.5*(1.0 - sqrt_disc); r2 = 1.0 - r1
    best=None; bestd=1e100
    hi1 = min(hi, r1)
    if lo <= hi1 + tol:
       x = clip_val(h0, lo, hi1)
       # ensure x <= r1+tol
       if x <= r1 + tol:
          d=abs(x-h0); update
    lo2 = max(lo, r2)
    if lo2 <= hi + tol:
       x = clip_val(h0, lo2, hi)
       if x >= r2 - tol: update
    if best is None:
       return clip_val(h0, lo, hi)
    return best
```
If h0 outside lo/hi, clip returns nearest. For Cq>=0.25, all [lo,hi]. Good.

def diag_segments(lo, hi, Cq):
    segs=[]
    lo=max(0,lo); hi=min(1,hi)
    if lo>hi: return segs
    if Cq is None or Cq >=0.25-1e-13:
       segs.append((lo,hi)); return segs
    if Cq <=1e-13:
       if lo <=1e-12 <=hi: segs.append((0.0,0.0))
       if lo <=1-1e-12 <=hi: segs.append((1.0,1.0))
       return segs
    r1=...; r2=...
    hi1=min(hi,r1)
    if lo <= hi1 + 1e-12: segs.append((lo,hi1))
    lo2=max(lo,r2)
    if lo2 <= hi + 1e-12: segs.append((lo2,hi))
    return segs
```

Now, code details for coordinate_descent:
```
def coordinate_descent(h0, c0, n, end_deadline, rng, random_order=False, damp=1.0):
    dx=2.0/n
    target=n/2.0
    h = np.array(h0, copy=True); c = np.array(c0, copy=True)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(np.max(c))*dx
    cur_damp = float(damp)
    no_improve=0
    min_damp=0.05
    while time.time() < end_deadline - 5.0:
        old_h = h.copy(); old_c = c.copy()
        start_c = float(np.max(c))*dx
        order = rng.permutation(n) if random_order else np.arange(n)
        changed=False
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > end_deadline - 8.0: break
            d = update_coord(c,h,p,n,cur_damp)
            if d != 0.0: changed=True
        if time.time() > end_deadline - 8.0:
            break
        h = project_sum_box(h, target)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        new_c = float(np.max(c))*dx
        if new_c < start_c - 1e-14:
            if new_c < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = new_c; best_h = h.copy()
            no_improve=0
            # If start_c was much larger, maybe increase damp? keep.
        else:
            h = old_h; c = old_c
            cur_damp = max(min_damp, cur_damp*0.5)
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3 and cur_damp <= min_damp + 1e-12:
                break
        if not changed:
            break
    return best_h, best_c
```
If accepted pass but new_c not improve best, we still keep current h/c (improved from start). If it later can improve, okay. But best_h maybe not copy of current if current >best. That's fine. At end return best only. But if current improved but not best? impossible if best global lower; current may be worse than best but improved from perturbed start. We might continue from current and eventually beat best. Good. However if current is worse than best and no progress, we return best. Good.

What if start_c is not equal to best_c and pass improves start but still worse than best; accepted. Could burn time. In restarts, okay. In main from best, start=best. Good.

Maybe if a pass improves by tiny but then next passes fail, no_improve resets; may wander. If new_c < start_c - 1e-14 but best not improved, current now slightly lower than start but still >best? If start was best, new<best, would update. If start was perturbed >best, no effect. Fine.

Potential performance: copying old_c every pass length 1399; okay. c array max etc.

Now, resample_h:
```
def resample_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    y_old = np.append(h_old, h_old[0])  # for interpolation wrap? h not periodic; maybe right=h_old[-1]
    x_old_ext = np.append(x_old, 2.0)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old_ext, np.append(h_old, h_old[-1]))
    return project_sum_box(y, n_new/2.0)
```
Since h at 2 maybe not equal h0, use linear to endpoint. Fine. Endpoint not included; at x=2, value h_old[-1]? Actually domain [0,2), no value at 2. For interpolation to x near 2, use last value. Good.

`make_initial` fallback:
```
def make_initial(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
    z = 0.5 + 0.3*np.sin(np.pi*x) + 0.1*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
    z += 0.05*rng.normal(0,1,n)
    return project_sum_box(z, n/2)
```

`diag_segments` needs Cq possibly None:
```
def diag_segments(lo, hi, Cq):
    lo = max(0.0, lo); hi = min(1.0, hi)
    segs=[]
    if lo > hi: return segs
    if Cq is None or Cq >= 0.25 - 1e-13:
       segs.append((lo,hi)); return segs
    if Cq <= 1e-12:
       if lo - 1e-12 <= 0.0 <= hi + 1e-12: segs.append((0.0,0.0))
       if lo - 1e-12 <= 1.0 <= hi + 1e-12: segs.append((1.0,1.0))
       return segs
    disc=1-4*Cq; if disc<0 disc=0
    r1=0.5*(1-math.sqrt(disc)); r2=1-r1
    hi1=min(hi,r1)
    if lo <= hi1 + 1e-12: segs.append((lo, hi1))
    lo2=max(lo,r2)
    if lo2 <= hi + 1e-12: segs.append((lo2, hi))
    return segs
```
If Cq negative but > -? bisection final feasibility would fail; if called with Cq negative, Cq<=1e-12 branch tries endpoints only, but constraint if Cq<0 only likely no solution; update earlier should check Cq < -1e-12 infeasible and return. In final, add:
```
if d_idx>=0:
   Cq = L - c_diag + h0*(1-h0)
   if Cq < -1e-12: return 0.0
else Cq=None
```

Now, update_coord code:
```
def update_coord(c,h,p,n,damp):
    h0=h[p]
    lag_lo = min(p-(n-1), -p)
    lag_hi = max(p, n-1-p)
    ms = np.arange(n-1+lag_lo, n-1+lag_hi+1)
    lag = ms - (n-1)
    a = np.zeros(ms.size)
    jf = p - lag
    vf = (jf>=0)&(jf<n)
    if vf.any(): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    jb = p + lag
    vb = (jb>=0)&(jb<n)
    if vb.any(): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
    d_idx=-1; c_diag=0.0
    zmask = (lag == 0)
    if zmask.any(): d_idx=int(np.nonzero(zmask)[0][0]); c_diag=c[ms[d_idx]]
    c_aff = c[ms]
    unaff=0.0
    if ms[0] > 0: unaff=float(np.max(c[:ms[0]]))
    last=ms[-1]
    if last < 2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[last+1:])))
    cur_max=float(np.max(c))
    Llo=unaff; Lhi=cur_max
    pos=a>1e-15; neg=a<-1e-15
    pos_c=c_aff[pos]; pos_a=a[pos]
    neg_c=c_aff[neg]; neg_a=a[neg]
    zero=~pos & ~neg
    zero_max=float(np.max(c_aff[zero])) if zero.any() else -np.inf
    for _ in range(32):
       L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       feasible=True
       if zero.any() and zero_max > L + 1e-12: feasible=False
       if feasible:
          hi_x=1.0; lo_x=0.0
          if pos.any(): hi_x=float(np.min(h0 + (L - pos_c)/pos_a))
          if neg.any(): lo_x=float(np.max(h0 + (L - neg_c)/neg_a))
          if hi_x < 0.0: hi_x = -0.1
          if lo_x > 1.0: lo_x = 1.1
          lo_x = max(0.0, lo_x); hi_x = min(1.0, hi_x)
          if lo_x > hi_x + 1e-13: feasible=False
       if feasible and d_idx>=0:
          Cq=L - c_diag + h0*(1-h0)
          if Cq < -1e-13:
             feasible=False
          elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-13:
             if Cq <= 1e-13:
                if not ((hi_x >= -1e-13 and lo_x <= 1e-13) or (hi_x >= 1.0-1e-13 and lo_x <= 1.0+1e-13)):
                    feasible=False
             else:
                disc=1-4*Cq; sqrtd=math.sqrt(disc) if disc>0 else 0.0
                r1=0.5*(1-sqrtd); r2=1-r1
                if not ((hi_x >= 0.0 and lo_x <= r1 + 1e-13) or (hi_x >= r2 - 1e-13 and lo_x <= 1.0)):
                    feasible=False
       if feasible: Lhi=L
       else: Llo=L
    L=Lhi
    # final linear interval
    hi_x=1.0; lo_x=0.0
    if zero.any() and zero_max > L + 1e-12: return 0.0
    if pos.any(): hi_x=float(np.min(h0 + (L - pos_c)/pos_a))
    if neg.any(): lo_x=float(np.max(h0 + (L - neg_c)/neg_a))
    if hi_x < 0.0: hi_x=-0.1
    if lo_x >1.0: lo_x=1.1
    lo_x=max(0,lo_x); hi_x=min(1,hi_x)
    if lo_x > hi_x + 1e-12: return 0.0
    if d_idx>=0:
       Cq=L - c_diag + h0*(1-h0)
       if Cq < -1e-12: return 0.0
    else: Cq=None
    segs=diag_segments(lo_x,hi_x,Cq)
    if not segs: return 0.0
    x_full=None
    if hi_x - lo_x > 1e-8:
       focus=max(0.0, cur_max - max(2.0, 0.05*cur_max))
       best_x=None; best_score=1e100; best_dist=1e100
       for (seglo,seghi) in segs:
          for xc in (seglo, seghi, clip_val(h0,seglo,seghi)):
             xx=clip_val(xc,lo_x,hi_x)
             # if no diag, or Cq feasible: candidates from segs, ok
             dx=xx-h0
             cnew = c_aff + a*dx
             if d_idx>=0:
                cnew[d_idx] = c_diag + a[d_idx]*dx - dx*dx
             excess = cnew - focus
             score = float(np.sum(np.maximum(excess,0.0)))
             dist=abs(xx-h0)
             if score < best_score - 1e-12 or (abs(score-best_score) <= 1e-12 and dist < best_dist):
                best_score=score; best_x=xx; best_dist=dist
       if best_x is not None: x_full=best_x
    if x_full is None:
       bestd=1e100
       for (seglo,seghi) in segs:
          xx=clip_val(h0,seglo,seghi); d=abs(xx-h0)
          if d < bestd: bestd=d; x_full=xx
    delta=damp*(x_full-h0)
    if abs(delta) < 1e-14: return 0.0
    c[ms] += a*delta
    if d_idx>=0: c[ms[d_idx]] -= delta*delta
    h[p] = h0 + delta
    return float(delta)
```

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); budget=float(budget_s)
    hard=t0+max(1.0,budget-3.0)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n=len(h)
    except Exception:
       n=700; h=make_initial(n,rng)
    if n < 2:
       return h,0.0,n
    h=project_sum_box(h,n/2.0)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    # phase helper
    def? no closures. We'll inline.
    end1=t0+0.38*budget
    if end1 > time.time()+10 and end1 < hard:
       h1,c1=coordinate_descent(h,c,n,end1,rng,False,1.0)
       if c1 < best_c:
          h=h1; best_c=compute_c5(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    end2=t0+0.62*budget
    if end2 > time.time()+15:
       h2,c2=coordinate_descent(h,c,n,min(end2,hard),rng,True,0.8)
       if c2<best_c: ...
    # restarts list
    amps=(0.02,0.05,0.01,0.08,0.03,0.005)
    for i,amp in enumerate(amps):
       rem=hard-time.time()
       if rem < 45: break
       phase=min(80.0, 0.22*rem)
       if i==0: phase=min(120.0,0.35*rem)
       hp=project_sum_box(h + rng.normal(0.0,amp,n), n/2.0)
       cp=np.correlate(hp,1-hp,'full')
       hr,cr=coordinate_descent(hp,cp,n,time.time()+phase,rng,(i%2==0),0.6)
       if cr<best_c: update
    # complement
    rem=hard-time.time()
    if rem>50:
       hc=project_sum_box(1.0-h, n/2.0)
       cc=compute_c5(hc,n)
       if cc<best_c: h=hc; best_c=cc; c=...
       phase=min(90,0.25*rem)
       hr,cr=coordinate_descent(hc, np.correlate(hc,1-hc,'full'), n, time.time()+phase, rng, False,0.8)
       if cr<best_c: update
    # binary
    rem=hard-time.time()
    if rem>60:
       # top n/2
       k=int(np.floor(n/2))
       hbin=np.zeros(n)
       if k>0: hbin[np.argsort(h)[::-1][:k]]=1.0
       if n - 2*k == 1: hbin[int(np.argmin(hbin))?])=0.5? target n/2 non-integer; project will adjust.
       hbin=project_sum_box(hbin,n/2.0)
       phase=min(90,0.25*rem)
       hr,cr=coordinate_descent(hbin, np.correlate(hbin,1-hbin,'full'), n, time.time()+phase, rng, True,1.0)
       if cr<best_c: update
    # multi-n
    for ntry in (500,900):
       rem=hard-time.time()
       if rem < 90 or ntry==n: continue
       phase=min(120,0.3*rem)
       htry=resample_h(h,n,ntry)
       ctry=np.correlate(htry,1-htry,'full')
       htry,ctry=coordinate_descent(htry,ctry,ntry,time.time()+phase,rng,True,0.8)
       if ctry < best_c - 1e-10:
          h=htry; n=ntry; best_c=ctry; c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    # final polish
    rem=hard-time.time()
    if rem>25:
       h=project_sum_box(h,n/2.0)
       c=...
       hf,cf=coordinate_descent(h,c,n,hard-2.0,rng,False,1.0)
       if cf<best_c: ...
    h=project_sum_box(h,n/2.0)
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    return h,best_c,n
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)

def clip_val(x, lo, hi):
    if x < lo:
        return lo
    if x > hi:
        return hi
    return x

def project_sum_box(x, target):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(100):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(40):
        d = target - y.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-14:
            break
        if d > 0.0:
            mask = y < 1.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            y[mask] += d / cnt
            y = np.minimum(y, 1.0)
        else:
            mask = y > 0.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            y[mask] += d / cnt
            y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    return y

def diag_segments(lo, hi, cq):
    lo = max(0.0, lo)
    hi = min(1.0, hi)
    segs = []
    if lo > hi:
        return segs
    if cq is None or cq >= 0.25 - 1e-13:
        segs.append((lo, hi))
        return segs
    if cq <= 1e-12:
        if lo - 1e-12 <= 0.0 <= hi + 1e-12:
            segs.append((0.0, 0.0))
        if lo - 1e-12 <= 1.0 <= hi + 1e-12:
            segs.append((1.0, 1.0))
        return segs
    disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * cq
    if disc < 0.0:
        disc = 0.0
    sd = math.sqrt(disc)
    r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - sd)
    r2 = 1.0 - r1
    hi1 = min(hi, r1)
    if lo <= hi1 + 1e-12:
        segs.append((lo, hi1))
    lo2 = max(lo, r2)
    if lo2 <= hi + 1e-12:
        segs.append((lo2, hi))
    return segs

def update_coord(c, h, p, n, damp):
    h0 = h[p]
    lag_lo = min(p - (n - 1), -p)
    lag_hi = max(p, n - 1 - p)
    ms = np.arange(n - 1 + lag_lo, n - 1 + lag_hi + 1)
    lag = ms - (n - 1)

    a = np.zeros(ms.size, dtype=float)
    jf = p - lag
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    if vf.any():
        a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
    jb = p + lag
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
    if vb.any():
        a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]

    d_idx = -1
    c_diag = 0.0
    zm = (lag == 0)
    if zm.any():
        d_idx = int(np.nonzero(zm)[0][0])
        c_diag = c[ms[d_idx]]

    c_aff = c[ms].copy()
    unaff = 0.0
    if ms[0] > 0:
        unaff = float(np.max(c[:ms[0]]))
    last = ms[-1]
    if last < 2 * n - 2:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[last + 1:])))
    cur_max = float(np.max(c))

    llo = unaff
    lhi = cur_max

    pos = a > 1e-15
    neg = a < -1e-15
    pos_c = c_aff[pos]
    pos_a = a[pos]
    neg_c = c_aff[neg]
    neg_a = a[neg]
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    zero_max = float(np.max(c_aff[zero])) if zero.any() else -np.inf

    for _ in range(30):
        L = 0.5 * (llo + lhi)
        feasible = True
        if zero.any() and zero_max > L + 1e-12:
            feasible = False
        if feasible:
            hi_x = 1.0
            lo_x = 0.0
            if pos.any():
                hi_x = float(np.min(h0 + (L - pos_c) / pos_a))
            if neg.any():
                lo_x = float(np.max(h0 + (L - neg_c) / neg_a))
            if hi_x < 0.0:
                hi_x = -0.1
            if lo_x > 1.0:
                lo_x = 1.1
            lo_x = max(0.0, lo_x)
            hi_x = min(1.0, hi_x)
            if lo_x > hi_x + 1e-13:
                feasible = False
        if feasible and d_idx >= 0:
            cq = L - c_diag + h0 * (1.0 - h0)
            if cq < -1e-13:
                feasible = False
            elif cq < 0.25 - 1e-13:
                if cq <= 1e-13:
                    if not ((hi_x >= -1e-13 and lo_x <= 1e-13) or (hi_x >= 1.0 - 1e-13 and lo_x <= 1.0 + 1e-13)):
                        feasible = False
                else:
                    disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * cq
                    if disc < 0.0:
                        disc = 0.0
                    sd = math.sqrt(disc)
                    r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - sd)
                    r2 = 1.0 - r1
                    if not ((hi_x >= 0.0 and lo_x <= r1 + 1e-13) or (hi_x >= r2 - 1e-13 and lo_x <= 1.0)):
                        feasible = False
        if feasible:
            lhi = L
        else:
            llo = L

    L = lhi

    hi_x = 1.0
    lo_x = 0.0
    if zero.any() and zero_max > L + 1e-12:
        return 0.0
    if pos.any():
        hi_x = float(np.min(h0 + (L - pos_c) / pos_a))
    if neg.any():
        lo_x = float(np.max(h0 + (L - neg_c) / neg_a))
    if hi_x < 0.0:
        hi_x = -0.1
    if lo_x > 1.0:
        lo_x = 1.1
    lo_x = max(0.0, lo_x)
    hi_x = min(1.0, hi_x)
    if lo_x > hi_x + 1e-12:
        return 0.0

    if d_idx >= 0:
        cq = L - c_diag + h0 * (1.0 - h0)
        if cq < -1e-12:
            return 0.0
    else:
        cq = None

    segs = diag_segments(lo_x, hi_x, cq)
    if not segs:
        return 0.0

    x_full = None
    if hi_x - lo_x > 1e-8:
        focus = max(0.0, cur_max - max(2.0, 0.05 * cur_max))
        base = c_aff - focus
        best_x = None
        best_score = 1e100
        best_dist = 1e100
        for seglo, seghi in segs:
            for xc in (seglo, seghi, clip_val(h0, seglo, seghi)):
                xx = clip_val(xc, lo_x, hi_x)
                dx = xx - h0
                t = base + a * dx
                if d_idx >= 0:
                    old = t[d_idx]
                    newval = c_diag + a[d_idx] * dx - dx * dx - focus
                    score = float(np.sum(np.maximum(t, 0.0)) - float(np.maximum(old, 0.0)) + float(np.maximum(newval, 0.0)))
                else:
                    score = float(np.sum(np.maximum(t, 0.0)))
                dist = abs(xx - h0)
                if score < best_score - 1e-12 or (abs(score - best_score) <= 1e-12 and dist < best_dist):
                    best_score = score
                    best_x = xx
                    best_dist = dist
        if best_x is not None:
            x_full = best_x

    if x_full is None:
        best_dist = 1e100
        for seglo, seghi in segs:
            xx = clip_val(h0, seglo, seghi)
            dist = abs(xx - h0)
            if dist < best_dist:
                best_dist = dist
                x_full = xx

    delta = damp * (x_full - h0)
    if abs(delta) < 1e-14:
        return 0.0

    c[ms] += a * delta
    if d_idx >= 0:
        c[ms[d_idx]] -= delta * delta
    h[p] = h0 + delta
    return float(delta)

def coordinate_descent(h0, c0, n, end_deadline, rng, random_order=False, damp=1.0):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    target = n / 2.0
    h = np.array(h0, copy=True)
    c = np.array(c0, copy=True)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(np.max(c)) * dx
    cur_damp = float(damp)
    min_damp = 0.05
    stagnant = 0

    while time.time() < end_deadline - 6.0:
        old_h = h.copy()
        old_c = c.copy()
        start_c = float(np.max(c)) * dx
        order = rng.permutation(n) if random_order else np.arange(n)
        changed = False
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > end_deadline - 8.0:
                break
            delta = update_coord(c, h, p, n, cur_damp)
            if delta != 0.0:
                changed = True

        if time.time() > end_deadline - 8.0:
            break

        h = project_sum_box(h, target)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        new_c = float(np.max(c)) * dx

        accepted = False
        improved = False
        if new_c < start_c - 1e-13:
            accepted = True
            improved = True
        elif new_c <= start_c + 1e-10 and new_c < best_c + 0.002:
            accepted = True

        if accepted:
            if improved:
                stagnant = 0
            else:
                stagnant += 1
            if new_c < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = new_c
                best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            h = old_h
            c = old_c
            cur_damp = max(min_damp, 0.5 * cur_damp)
            stagnant += 2
            if stagnant > 5 and cur_damp <= min_damp + 1e-12:
                break

        if not changed:
            break

    return best_h, best_c

def make_initial(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    z = 0.5 + 0.30 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.10 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    z += 0.04 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return project_sum_box(z, n / 2.0)

def resample_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    x_ext = np.append(x_old, 2.0)
    y_ext = np.append(h_old, h_old[-1])
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_ext, y_ext)
    return project_sum_box(y, n_new / 2.0)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    hard = t0 + max(1.0, budget - 3.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h))
    except Exception:
        n = 700
        h = make_initial(n, rng)

    if n < 2:
        return h, 0.0, n

    h = project_sum_box(h, n / 2.0)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    end1 = min(hard, t0 + 0.38 * budget)
    if end1 > time.time() + 10.0:
        h1, c1 = coordinate_descent(h, c, n, end1, rng, False, 1.0)
        if c1 < best_c:
            h = h1
            best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

    end2 = min(hard, t0 + 0.62 * budget)
    if end2 > time.time() + 15.0:
        h2, c2 = coordinate_descent(h, c, n, end2, rng, True, 0.8)
        if c2 < best_c:
            h = h2
            best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

    amps = (0.02, 0.05, 0.01, 0.08, 0.03, 0.005)
    for i, amp in enumerate(amps):
        rem = hard - time.time()
        if rem < 45.0:
            break
        phase = min(80.0, 0.22 * rem)
        if i == 0:
            phase = min(120.0, 0.35 * rem)
        hp = project_sum_box(h + rng.normal(0.0, amp, n), n / 2.0)
        cp = np.correlate(hp, 1.0 - hp, mode="full")
        hr, cr = coordinate_descent(hp, cp, n, time.time() + phase, rng, (i % 2 == 0), 0.6)
        if cr < best_c:
            h = hr
            best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

    rem = hard - time.time()
    if rem > 55.0:
        hc = project_sum_box(1.0 - h, n / 2.0)
        cc_arr = np.correlate(hc, 1.0 - hc, mode="full")
        cc = float(np.max(cc_arr)) * (2.0 / n)
        if cc < best_c:
            h = hc
            best_c = cc
        ctry = np.correlate(hc, 1.0 - hc, mode="full")
        phase = min(90.0, 0.25 * (hard - time.time()))
        htry, ctry = coordinate_descent(hc, ctry, n, time.time() + phase, rng, False, 0.8)
        if ctry < best_c:
            h = htry
            best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

    rem = hard - time.time()
    if rem > 65.0:
        k = int(n // 2)
        hbin = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        if k > 0:
            idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1][:k]
            hbin[idx] = 1.0
        hbin = project_sum_box(hbin, n / 2.0)
        cbin = np.correlate(hbin, 1.0 - hbin, mode="full")
        phase = min(90.0, 0.25 * (hard - time.time()))
        hb, cb = coordinate_descent(hbin, cbin, n, time.time() + phase, rng, True, 1.0)
        if cb < best_c:
            h = hb
            best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

    for ntry in (500, 900):
        rem = hard - time.time()
        if rem < 95.0 or ntry == n:
            continue
        htry = resample_h(h, n, ntry)
        ctry = np.correlate(htry, 1.0 - htry, mode="full")
        phase = min(120.0, 0.30 * rem)
        htry, ctry = coordinate_descent(htry, ctry, ntry, time.time() + phase, rng, True, 0.8)
        if ctry < best_c - 1e-10:
            h = htry
            n = ntry
            best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")

    rem = hard - time.time()
    if rem > 25.0:
        h = project_sum_box(h, n / 2.0)
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        hf, cf = coordinate_descent(h, c, n, max(time.time() + 10.0, hard - 2.0), rng, False, 1.0)
        if cf < best_c:
            h = hf
            best_c = cf

    h = project_sum_box(h, n / 2.0)
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, best_c, n
```
